## 2026-09-04 — The audits are measuring yesterday's corpus: today's 'unchanged' 47% reading was taken 90 seconds before the pipeline rebuilt the pages it was reading
*6am review*

**Observed.** YESTERDAY'S PRE-REGISTERED TEST APPEARED TO FAIL, AND THE FAILURE WAS THE INSTRUMENT. 09-03 shipped distance-ordered sibling blocks to the SF/LA/DC hub tier, measured /dc/neighborhood/ duplicate share falling 46.7% -> 37.5% from this checkout, and pre-registered 'dc reads 36-39% tomorrow', saying a reading near 47% would mean the change never reached the docroot. Today t_page_uniqueness reported /dc/neighborhood/ 47% of 472 words (n=24 of 103) — BYTE-IDENTICAL to yesterday's string — while the SEO pipeline logged 305 changed URLs (up from 177) and 'ran clean (synced-2)'. Both are true at once, because of the ORDER: growth_run.sh runs build --deploy first (05:40:30, all eleven techniques) and only THEN fires the SEO watchdog, which ran refresh_seo.sh 05:40:30-05:41:57. The audit finished reading ninety seconds BEFORE the corpus it audits was rewritten. VERIFIED THREE WAYS, not inferred: (a) re-rendered the tier from this checkout's own build_seo.py and got dc 37.5% / 528 words, sf 14.9% / 1,518, la 20.1% / 1,408 — the code is right and shipped; (b) built a 255-page synthetic docroot from it and ran the PRODUCTION t_page_uniqueness over it, which returned '/dc/neighborhood/ 38% of 528 words' — inside yesterday's 36-39% band; (c) read growth_run.sh, which invokes seo-status after the watchdog to patch seo_corpus and seo_pipeline and NOTHING ELSE. THE INDEX CENSUS, ELEVENTH FLAT DAY: cohort 455, fetched 62 mature (20.9%), indexed 1 (0.2%), accept_pct 1.1, accept_pct_mature 0.0%, 28d+ bucket 0 of 49, states crawled_not_indexed 94 / discovered_not_indexed 3 / indexed 1 / unknown_to_google 357. SEARCH: gsc_serving_pages 5 (5 yesterday, 8 on 09-01, 63 on 08-20), serving_ever 277 FLAT FOR THE SEVENTH DAY, serving_stable 5, clicks 67 of which nonbranded 0 for the SEVENTH day, nonbranded_impressions 0, share_pct 0.0, tracked_ranking 0 of 390, coverage 82.3%. REVENUE: $0/mo, paying_subs 0, reports_sold 0 — day 39 of the $9 report. CRONS ALL FIRED: daily 05:00-05:19 rc=0, build --deploy 05:40 rc=0, watchdog ok, report 06:00 rc=0. ANTHROPIC FUNDED, SECOND DAY: scout ok=true (3 techniques, 25 keywords, T076-T078), outreach ok=true 0 drafted — neither the empty balance nor the console cap. YESTERDAY'S changed: LINE CHECKS OUT — _city_centroid, _miles and _city_neighbors are all in build_seo.py beside _city_stats, the block is headed 'Nearest neighborhoods in Washington, DC' with the mid-point disclaimer, exactly as claimed.

**Concluded.** EVERY DOCROOT AUDIT THIS SITE RUNS IS A FULL DAY BEHIND ANY build_seo.py CHANGE THE REVIEW LOOP PUSHES, AND I ALMOST REVERTED A GOOD FIX BECAUSE OF IT. This is not an edge case: the 04:10 pipeline cron has not fired since 2026-08-14, so the watchdog is not a repair path, it IS how the 47,599 pages get built — every morning, after the audits have already read yesterday's copy. So the lag is not occasional, it is universal and permanent. It is the exact twin of the bug cmd_seo_status was written for on 2026-08-20, when last_run.json was structurally guaranteed to report a dead pipeline on the mornings the watchdog worked; that fix corrected two fields and left every technique record uncorrected, and nobody noticed for a fortnight because the readings were plausible. WORSE, _stamp_unchanged MAKES A STALE READING LOOK LIKE A STABLE ONE — city_seo_expansion, hub_direct_answers and city_guides are all stamped 'unchanged since 2026-09-02', which reads as 'healthy and steady' and actually means 'this instrument has not looked since'. I SHOULD SAY PLAINLY THAT YESTERDAY'S ENTRY WROTE THE WRONG DEBUGGING ADVICE. It said: if dc comes back near 47%, check the seo pipeline line in last_run.json before blaming the measurement. That check would have PASSED today — the pipeline line says 'ran clean', 305 changed — and a reader following it would then have concluded the change itself failed and reverted a fix that had worked. t_page_uniqueness's own docstring already warned about this lag two days ago, and the warning did not save anyone, which is the lesson: a caveat in a docstring does not reach the person reading a detail string six weeks later. Fix the instrument, don't document the flaw. T037 crawl_paths CAME DUE AND ITS HYPOTHESIS IS FALSIFIED AS A CAUSAL CLAIM. The linking half was fully delivered — 15 of 15 sections have an inbound link, all within 3 clicks of the homepage — and indexing went the OTHER WAY over the 30 days since: serving_pages 63 -> 5, index_state_indexed 10 -> 1, accept_pct_mature 50% -> 0.0%. An inbound link is necessary and was not the binding constraint. Its 'WORKS' verdict is an artifact of its metric: review.py credits it with organic_visitors 9.5/day vs 2.5/day while gsc_nonbranded_clicks has been 0 for seven days against 67 branded, so it is being credited with jayshomefinder residue it cannot have caused. KEPT ANYWAY, and the reason is not sentiment — it is read-only, it is the guard that stops the next section shipping into an orphan hole, and its click-depth and 'published under no ACTIVE technique' lines are what the duplication work is steering by. TODAY'S RESEARCH CORROBORATES THE DUPLICATION THESIS BUT CHANGES NOTHING, and I am flagging its provenance rather than leaning on it: the 2026 material converges on site-wide quality thresholds — mass 'crawled, currently not indexed' as a signal of doubts about the whole site, with near-identical programmatic pages named as a trigger — which is the same account page_uniqueness was built to test. These are SEO blogs. THE noindex,follow QUESTION IS STILL NOT SETTLED: I searched for a Google primary source and did not find one; what came back was blog consensus that noindexed pages do not affect crawl budget, which is a different question from whether they count against site quality. Do not act on it. STILL BROKEN, NOT MINE: AdSense has no slot id so no ads render; api@findacrib.com may not be a live forwarding alias.

**Changed.** MADE THE POST-REFRESH RE-READ PART OF THE PIPELINE, so an audit can no longer report on a corpus that has since been rebuilt. Added techniques.DOCROOT_VERIFIERS = ('derived_building_facts', 'page_uniqueness') with the membership rule written next to it: a technique qualifies only if it touches nothing but ctx.docroot — no write_page/write_raw/unstage, no ledger.set_state, no network — so re-running it is free of side effects. Added growth_daily._reread_docroot_verifiers(), called from cmd_seo_status, which runs those audits again against the docroot the watchdog has just rebuilt and patches the changed records into last_run.json's build.techniques. DELIBERATE CHOICES, each for a reason already paid for in this journal: it returns None on every failure path and catches each audit separately, so a crash here costs the corpus correction nothing and one unreadable section cannot strand the other audit's fresh reading; `ok` is written explicitly on every record, per 2026-07-28, so no record can be announced as 'DID NOT RUN — unknown error'; the context is dry_run=True as belt-and-braces, not a behaviour switch, so a future member that quietly writes cannot deploy pages from a correction pass; and stamps are handled honestly — a re-read that AGREES with the pre-refresh reading keeps the build's own `unchanged`/`same_since` verbatim, while one that differs drops `unchanged` and dates `same_since` today, which is what _stamp_unchanged would have concluded had it been able to read the rebuilt corpus. WHY ONLY TWO MEMBERS: t_crawl_paths and t_hub_direct_answers are equally wrong-by-a-day, but both consult ctx.out for staged-not-yet-rsynced pages and t_crawl_paths records first-sighting dates through ledger.set_state, so re-running them is NOT free. Making them pure is a separate change and I did not stack it on top of this one. TESTED, not assumed: built a 255-page synthetic docroot from this checkout's build_seo.py and confirmed the production audit returns dc 38% / 528 words there — so had this shipped a day earlier, today's record would have read 38%, inside yesterday's pre-registered band, instead of the stale 47%; exercised BOTH branches — a differing reading replaces the record, drops `unchanged` and dates `same_since`, with all nine neighbouring technique records byte-identical afterwards; an agreeing reading returns None and patches nothing. T037 REVISIT: kept, notes rewritten in growth/seed.py (NOT techniques.json — seed.run() overwrites notes from seed.py every build, so a note written into the ledger would have been silently reverted tomorrow) recording the falsification with its figures, the mis-attributed verdict, and the concrete next step of making it a pure reader; revisit_on pushed 2026-09-04 -> 2026-10-04. ADDED NO KEYWORDS: 390 tracked, 0 ranking, 82.3% covered — the query universe has never been the constraint. RETIRED NOTHING. Did NOT run keywords.check_coverage(). VERIFIED: py_compile clean on growth/*.py, growth_daily.py and build_seo.py; 'growth_daily.py build --dry-run --docroot . --build-dir .' exits 0; 'seo-status --dry-run' exits 0 and patches only the two verifier records; techniques.json diff is two lines, crawl_paths notes and revisit_on, no other slug touched.

**Watching.** (1) THE REAL TEST OF YESTERDAY'S HUB CHANGE FINALLY LANDS TOMORROW, and this time the instrument can answer it. Prediction on record so it can be wrong: page_uniqueness reports /dc/neighborhood/ at 37-39% of ~510-530 words, la ~20%, sf ~15%, and the record carries reread:'post-seo-refresh' with same_since 2026-09-05. If dc still reads 47%/472 the re-read did not fire — check for the 'verifier re-read' lines and whether cmd_seo_status ran at all, because seo-status is invoked with output swallowed and a failure there is silent by design. If it reads 38% with NO reread stamp, the pre-refresh reading had already caught up and the patch correctly stood down. (2) HOW MANY OTHER 'unchanged since' STAMPS ARE STALENESS RATHER THAN STABILITY. city_seo_expansion, hub_direct_answers, city_guides, llms_txt, sitemap_daily and indexnow all read 'unchanged, same_since 2026-09-02'. The first three are docroot audits and are day-late by the same mechanism; they cannot join DOCROOT_VERIFIERS until they stop writing. Treat any 'unchanged' on a docroot audit as unverified until then. (3) accept_pct_mature and index_state_indexed — eleven days at 0.0% and one indexed page. Still the number everything else is upstream of, and nothing shipped today can move it: this run fixed a measurement, not a cause. (4) gsc_serving_ever, seventh flat day at 277. It cannot fall, so seven flat days say the intake is closed rather than slow. (5) gsc_nonbranded_clicks, seven days at 0 against 67 branded. (6) /available/ IS STILL THE PRE-REGISTERED NEXT SECTION and I left it a second day: 90 pages, 42% shared of only 211 words (up from 38% of 230), published under NO active technique so nothing rebuilds it, and 0 of 15 sampled URLs ever fetched. Thin, duplicated, unmaintained, never crawled — the question is consolidate or stop, not improve. (7) T037's mis-attributed verdict is a pattern, not a one-off: review.py credits every technique with a site-wide metric, so any technique active during the branded-click drift will read 'WORKS'. Check whether T003/T004/T005/T010 are earning their verdicts the same way. (8) THE noindex,follow QUESTION IS UNCHANGED and still needs a Google primary source. (9) Unchanged: subtract findacrib and jayshomefinder from every raw click count, and treat every serving_pages figure as a floor.


## 2026-09-05 — Seven active techniques were being judged on one site-wide number, and four of them were carrying a WORKS verdict off the same 11-vs-6 lift
*6am review*

**Observed.** YESTERDAY'S PRE-REGISTERED TEST PASSED, AND IT IS THE FIRST CLEAN PASS THIS LOOP HAS RECORDED. 09-04 shipped growth_daily._reread_docroot_verifiers() so the docroot audits re-run AFTER the SEO watchdog rebuilds the corpus, and predicted page_uniqueness would report /dc/neighborhood/ at 37-39% of ~510-530 words with the record stamped reread:'post-seo-refresh'. Today: '/dc/neighborhood/ 38% of 528 words (n=24 of 103)', reread 'post-seo-refresh', same_since 2026-09-05, la /la/zip/ 20% of 1,408 (predicted ~20%). The 09-03 hub change was real all along and the 47% reading on 09-04 was the instrument reading yesterday's corpus, exactly as diagnosed. YESTERDAY'S changed: LINE ALSO CHECKS OUT — techniques.DOCROOT_VERIFIERS at techniques.py:2618, _reread_docroot_verifiers defined at growth_daily.py:381 and called from cmd_seo_status at :369, crawl_paths notes rewritten in seed.py and revisit_on 2026-10-04. SEARCH, STILL FALLING: gsc_serving_pages 4 (5, 5, 6, 8, 8, 13, 11, 15 on the eight days before; 63 on 08-20), serving_stable 4, serving_ever 277 FLAT FOR THE EIGHTH DAY, tracked_ranking 0 of 440, share_pct 0.0. Clicks 71 of 87 impressions — an 81% CTR, which is what a navigational query looks like: nonbranded_clicks 0 and nonbranded_impressions 0 for the EIGHTH day. INDEX CENSUS, TWELFTH FLAT DAY: cohort 455, fetched_mature 64 (20.9%), indexed 1 (0.2%), accept_pct 1.1, accept_pct_mature 0.0%, 28d+ bucket 0 of 51, states crawled_not_indexed 94 / indexed 1 / unknown_to_google 360. REVENUE /bin/bash/mo, paying_subs 0, reports_sold 0 — day 40 of the $9 report. CRONS ALL FIRED: daily 05:00-05:19 rc=0, build --deploy 05:40 rc=0, watchdog ran refresh_seo.sh to completion rc=0, seo pipeline 'ran clean (synced-1)' 49,374 pages / 19 changed. ANTHROPIC FUNDED, THIRD DAY: scout ok=true (3 techniques, 25 keywords), outreach ok=true 0 drafted — neither the empty balance nor the console cap. THE FINDING: grouping the ACTIVE ledger by declared metric, SEVEN techniques with no prefixes of their own are all judged on the single site-wide organic_visitors series — T004 sitemap_daily, T005 indexnow, T010 gsc_integration, T015 dataset_schema_ai_citation, T016 embeddable_building_lookup_widget, T037 crawl_paths, T072 page_uniqueness — and TWO on reports_sold (T011, T017). Four of the seven were carrying WORKS quoting the same 'organic_visitors median 11.0/day vs 6.0/day [155 in the last 14d]' string. VERIFIED the index triage is NOT the cause of the serving collapse and is working as designed: from this checkout, _ever_served_bbls() extracts 264 BBLs from growth/gsc_pages.json history, all 264 are present in buildings.min.json (0 missing), all 312 advertised BBLs are present, and promoted_building() promotes 936 of 47,165 (2.0%). The decline also began 08-20 to 08-27 (63 to 26), BEFORE the triage first deployed.

**Concluded.** THE LOOP HAS BEEN CREDITING SEVEN TECHNIQUES WITH ONE NUMBER, AND THE NUMBER IS BRANDED TRAFFIC. Yesterday's entry caught this on T037 and asked the next review to check whether T003/T004/T005/T010 were earning verdicts the same way. They were, and it is worse than four: seven active techniques declare organic_visitors and none of them declares any prefixes, so review.evaluate() compares every one of them against the identical __site__ series and hands the same movement to all of them. They cannot all have caused it. Worse, the movement itself is almost certainly not theirs at all: gsc_nonbranded_clicks has read 0 for eight consecutive days while gsc_clicks climbed 3 to 71, so the organic rise the four WORKS verdicts are quoting is jayshomefinder and findacrib residue — the exact residue this journal has instructed every review to subtract by hand for weeks. This is the same class of defect as PAID_FLIGHT_DAYS (a distortion known in prose and not encoded in the instrument) and the same class as yesterday's stale-audit fix, and it is the more dangerous one, because a false WORKS does not sit still: scout.py feeds the WORKS list into the prompt that proposes tomorrow's techniques, so it breeds, and it tells a future review a question is settled when it is not. I AM CORRECTING THE RECORD ON T037: its 2026-09-04 'kept' decision stands on its own merits (it is a read-only guard and its click-depth output is steering the duplication work), but its WORKS verdict was never evidence for anything and is now withdrawn. THE INDEX TRIAGE IS NOT THE STORY I EXPECTED IT TO BE. I went looking for it as the cause of serving_pages 63 to 4, given the 08-28 to 08-30 window when EVER_SERVED_BBLS was empty on production, and measured it instead: 264 of 264 ever-served BBLs resolve into the corpus, 312 of 312 advertised, 936 promoted. The rule works and the decline predates it. Eight flat days on serving_ever, a counter that cannot fall, say Google's intake is closed rather than slow, and 0.0% acceptance on 64 mature-fetched pages says the corpus is being declined on quality, not on crawl access. I ALSO OVERTURN THE LAST TWO ENTRIES' PRE-REGISTRATION ON /available/, and the reason is a fact neither of them had. Both said the question was 'consolidate or stop, not improve' on the grounds that it is thin (211 words), duplicated (42%), never crawled (0 of 15 sampled ever fetched) and 'published under no ACTIVE technique'. But advertised-right-now is the FIRST rule in build_seo.py's promoted_building(), so the 312 buildings /available/ browses are the core of the 936-page promoted tier, and refresh_seo.sh deploys with NO --delete — so 'stop publishing' cannot remove anything from the docroot, it can only strand 90 pages nothing rebuilds, which is strictly the condition crawl_paths is complaining about. Both halves of the plan were wrong. It is a browse surface for the promoted tier and it stays; T012 listings_freshness is the ledger record that owns it and it is still a candidate. TODAY'S RESEARCH ADDS NOTHING TO THE LEDGER and I am flagging its provenance rather than leaning on it: the 2026 material again converges on site-wide quality thresholds and near-identical programmatic templates, which is what page_uniqueness already tests, plus one claim worth noting only because it names an existing candidate — that 90-95% of AI citations come from third-party sources, which is T047's whole premise. These are SEO and AEO marketing blogs quoting their own numbers. STILL BROKEN, NOT MINE: AdSense has no slot id so no ads render; api@findacrib.com may not be a live forwarding alias.

**Changed.** ADDED AN ATTRIBUTION GUARD TO growth/review.py so the loop can no longer credit one technique with a site-wide movement several techniques are claiming at once. New _co_claimants(t, metric, techs) returns the other ACTIVE, prefix-less techniques declaring the same metric; new _guard_shared_metric(res, metric, co) demotes works=True to works=None and appends to the why string the count and the first three co-claimants by id and slug. evaluate() takes an optional techs argument (optional so a caller holding one record can still evaluate it; with nothing passed the guard simply cannot fire) and run() passes the ledger it already loaded. Wired into BOTH site-wide paths — the flow verdict and the _judge_stock return — because reports_sold is shared by T011 and T017 exactly as organic_visitors is shared by seven. THREE DELIBERATE CHOICES. (1) ONLY works=True IS DEMOTED, never a flag or a retirement, and the asymmetry is the same one MIN_CENSUS_READ is built on: a false WORKS propagates into scout.py's prompt and into every future review, a false 'no lift' costs one flag a person reads and dismisses, so guard the direction that breeds. (2) NOTHING TOUCHES res['action'] — verified, no technique's status, activated or retired field changed — so a demoted technique keeps running and keeps being measured; what stops is the ledger asserting a causal claim the numbers cannot support. (3) NO DATE ARITHMETIC: every claimant is live today, so their trailing windows are identical by construction and sharing the metric IS the overlap; a technique with its own prefixes is judged on the owned path and never reaches here, so it is correctly not counted. The full co-claimant list is kept in measured['co_claimants'] even though the why string names only three, so the evidence survives in the ledger while the email stays readable. TESTED, not assumed: review.run(apply=False) before and after. T004, T005, T010 and T037 flip WORKS to UNPROVEN with the co-claimant list attached; T003 llms_txt (sole claimant on ai_visitors) and T019 hub_direct_answers (owned path) are untouched and still WORKS; T008, T011, T017 keep their existing flags and verdicts; redundant is empty and the action list is byte-identical to before. Then ran review.run(apply=True) so the ledger stops asserting the false claim today rather than tomorrow — the techniques.json diff is verdict blocks only, no status line touched. ADDED NO KEYWORDS: 440 tracked, 0 ranking, 82.4% covered — the query universe has never been the constraint. RETIRED NOTHING. NOTHING WAS DUE FOR REVISIT (next: T015 and T016 on 2026-09-12). Did NOT run keywords.check_coverage(). VERIFIED: py_compile clean on growth/*.py, growth_daily.py and build_seo.py; 'growth_daily.py build --dry-run --docroot . --build-dir .' exits 0; report.build() renders and both the HTML and the plain-text part read correctly with the longer why strings.

**Watching.** (1) THE SCOREBOARD SHOULD LOSE FOUR WORKS TOMORROW. Prediction on record so it can be wrong: the daily report's SCOREBOARD SO FAR table shows WORKS for T019 and T003 only, and T004, T005, T010 and T037 move to UNPROVEN with a why ending 'so the movement cannot be attributed to any one of them'. If any of the four still reads WORKS, the droplet's review ran from code that is not this commit — check the daily cron's pull line in last_run.json before blaming the guard. (2) WHETHER THE GUARD IS TOO BLUNT. Seven techniques sharing organic_visitors means NO site-wide SEO technique can ever earn a WORKS again while that pile stands. That is honest, but it makes the metric useless for judging, and the real fix is upstream: these techniques need owned prefixes or their own measurable series, not a share of a site-wide number. The next review that touches this should ask whether T004/T005/T015/T016 can declare prefixes at all, and whether organic_visitors should be replaced with a nonbranded-only series now that gsc_nonbranded_clicks exists and has read 0 for eight days. (3) accept_pct_mature and index_state_indexed — twelve days at 0.0% and one indexed page. Still the number everything else is upstream of, and nothing shipped today can move it: this run fixed a measurement, not a cause. Two runs in a row have now fixed instruments; if a third does the same, that is a review loop polishing its own mirror instead of doing the work. (4) gsc_serving_ever, eighth flat day at 277, and gsc_serving_pages down 63 to 4 in sixteen days. (5) /available/ IS SETTLED AND SHOULD BE DROPPED FROM THE WATCH LIST — see today's conclusion. What is NOT settled is the 46,229 building pages carrying noindex,follow that are still fully linked from every /neighborhood/, /zip/ and /available/ hub: the triage removed them from the sitemap but did not reduce the crawl surface the internal links present, and build_seo.py's own comment says that is deliberate. Whether it should stay deliberate is a real question with the census at 0.0% mature acceptance. (6) THE noindex,follow QUESTION IS UNCHANGED and still needs a Google primary source, not blog consensus.
